The rehearsal runs end to end on scratch roots, on the real doors and on standing vessels of the built tree. --memory lands the bundle's content keys through the island's import door; where no session signer stands (a vessel at the floor) it refuses signer-unavailable. * The custody door and its census: lares vault answers seven verbs (status · unlock · lock · bind · unbind · backup · restore), and a credential rides a terminal alone: no flag, environment variable or pipe carries one. vault status gives ONE reading of each standing file, derived from the carrier table: a hot or cold row reads sealed or reads as a fault, a row outside the S key prints the law it rests bare under or reads as a fault where the file breaks that law, and a private file in the three homes that no row names prints by name (vault-carriers.ts outsideTheTable; custody-session.ts custodyStatus). vault unbind --rotate runs as one act: the rotation's census and its opening of every carrier pass before any byte moves, so a carrier that refuses the rotation leaves the slot bound. tools/custody-census-witness.sh founds a vessel on a marked scratch root with the built tree and refuses an owner-only file, a seal or a sidecar outside the table; it carries that rotation to its end on the founded vessel. The law, each bare row's reason and the residue a row's name leaves at rest read at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/custody#/the-table .

3 · HARBOR ~ the container's tail

  • A persona door before up: tools/lares-container.sh routes CLI acts through the offline door before up; after a successful act in a standing vessel the host shim reads the standing verdict and restarts the vessel on restand. The verdict reads a LIT face alone, so a persona wear that changes the worn face on a standing vessel waits on ONE FACE.
  • LAN candidate discovery: the module and wiring stand off by default; LAR_LAN_DISCOVERY=announce enables announcements without keys. A real-LAN run and the network mode remain owed (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/harbor#/lan-discovery).
  • Household HTTPS: the compose https profile supplies a terminator and a resolver slot, the issuer profile supplies a one-shot DNS-01 issuer that reads the registrar's credential from two owner-read files outside both mounts (docker-compose.surface.yml; tools/household-https-witness.sh walks issuance against a test authority), the resolver answers each household name with its own address from one records file the operator keeps (tools/household-resolver-witness.sh walks it against a test zone), and deviceAdmitSyncUrl derives relay reach from LAR_PUBLIC_URL. The node entry names itself by that setting alone: a boot without one, or with one that names loopback, says so in one loud line, and the first request that arrives under another host logs once per boot (packages/lararium-node/src/reach-face-watch.ts). The operator supplies the domain and the credential files. Owed: the renewal verb with its reload, and a first live issuance. No device takes an install (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/harbor#/household-https, lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/secure-context).
  • The harnesses stand on the image: harnesses run the built image by digest and bind no source tree; the mesh scenarios name their own compose project (MESH_PROJECT) and feed their browser vessels from the image; the browser CI runner alone reads the repository read-only. Each harness opens the vessel it staged through the test custody door and reads a hearth as stood only once that door answered an open key; mesh-scenarios all, harbor-walk-witness, the crossing and herm witnesses, the browser walks and the household HTTPS witness all stand on that shape (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/harbor#/the-harnesses).
  • The release manifest: tools/release-manifest.mjs emits the record shape; a real multi-arch index, its push and signing remain owed.

4 · KAHU PLANE ~ the realms, the signer, and the one AID train

Build order: the realm and signer shapes against the rulings; then R-CHARTER, the one train that moves identity bytes; then R-WIRE, so every pin changes once.

  • Realms: a vessel stands one plane per realm, and a verb names its realm ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/cabal-realm#/many-realms ). A cabal reads as its relation closure and takes no key or roster of its own. A cabal realm's documents cross between two operators once the realm's member consult answers for a peer.
  • The steward signer: the worn face or a quorum Handle, never the root ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/cabal-realm#/the-steward-signer ). The seal, the fold and the steward door stand in code; the composing vessel's realm-bag verb takes the door through the compose owner's hand. The docker meme scenario then proves it by hand at "B edits, promotes", in the asker's order: B asks to keep the bag with her worn face, and A consents.
  • Thresholded admission and promotion stand built: the chartered classes derive from the charter's own seats and threshold (chartered-classes.ts); promotion approves the exact bytes' hash with purpose, surface, class and epoch (promotion-approval.ts, lares nexus promote open|sign|check); Kahu-cabal admission stands as its own act at the high-impact threshold, where a vouch admits nobody (kahu-cabal-admission.ts, lares nexus cabal) ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/kahu#/chartered-classes ).

R-CHARTER, the one AID train. Its first stage stands landed (train K of #/the-trains): the chairs sign with seat keys of their own and pre-rotate on cold cards, a Nexus answers to nexus-<sha>, the floor key founds by a clockless self-signed inception (its next cold key committed since W29), per-Nexus keys mint at random, the PersonaGroup stands as a Keyhive group, and the Stable founding act founds a hearth (lares vessel found --hearth): the place, persona zero and a PRIVATE seed charter at one seat, with no keyring, every fleet relation bound to that charter's AID, and every harness that founds a hearth founding through it ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/founding-runbook#/found ). What stays owed: * The Nexus founding act: two founders, each from their own vault, seat one key each in a fresh joint genesis at two of two; each reads the other's seat fingerprint aloud; each seat commits its own cold next digest; each founder then signs a carriage contract that both seats countersign ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/kahu#/seed-and-nexus ). Train N. * The cadence inception cited once by a joint Nexus founding, never by a household seed: its shape stands in lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/kahu#/contract , and its holders stand ruled (#/rulings; K9). * Boards resolve per relation; the fleet-join door carries a device edge and one face's grants; add-a-face (A); the bare primary-path seal import goes; the mirror guard keys on posture.

R-WIRE, the per-Nexus wire key (waits on R-CHARTER). * A per-Nexus wire key in two tiers (operator ruling): a floor route's bundle derives from the floor key and answers at a locked vessel; a sovereign route's bundle derives from the sealed custody root and stands only while the vessel stands unlocked. One gate per (Nexus, route), so knock, pin, verdict and posture read per Nexus exactly ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/face-proof#/the-gate-exchange ). The serving route's third tier rides train E (E3). * Noise IK, proof-first: the gate emits nothing before a valid first message; the knock from gateKnock and knockSegment in gate-bundle.ts selects the sealed face gate, and Noise IK carries message one. * The floor announcement signs under the wire key, folded in here, so gate and announce never sign under two keys. * One client dial per (hearth, Nexus), so a vessel walks at more than one hearth; until then a second walk hearth refuses aloud. * The vessel key stops linking a walker's leaves across Nexuses on the wire (two-maps clause 5's residual), and the oracle pointer's vessel-key correlation goes with it (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/pronaos#/the-rung-ladder). * Per-dial transport keys on the sibling channel: a leaf proves to a herm under a key fresh per dial, and the leaf-peer transcript binds that key in place of the device key the relay stamps, so a herm colluding with a revoked leaf reads no sibling's device key off its own gate (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/identity-slot-policy#/the-persona-group-secret). * The admit signature the build owes: the canon-document list and full sovereign sync GateBundlePin enter the signed bytes through the fleet answer and admit carriage; device-admit.ts mints the internal device-admit edge. The browser's first proof rides Sec-WebSocket-Protocol (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/face-proof). * Owed (b), the knock tests offline: the knock path computes an HMAC-derived segment (packages/lararium-mesh/src/gate-bundle.ts, knockSegment, gateKnock); an observer holding candidate keys tests a sniffed knock offline, so not echoing the key protects only against an observer with no candidates.

Behind the train, each waiting on R-CHARTER. * R-KAHU stands witnessed: a two-of-two charter whose seats live on two vessels opens, cosigns and lands a cabal admission and a promotion, each vessel under its own scratch root (nexus-cabal.test.ts, nexus-promote.test.ts). The co-signed keptBy fills the realm's steward map (#/rulings). * R-ENGINE, the offers: the engine-offer lineage signs under the cadence threshold, adopted within a k-hop window along prev. * R-EMBER: a household's second device lifts a resting hearth to SERVING and to nothing more ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/ember ). It needs the fleet-join door; the slot tree carries passphrase and paper pins, and the ember pin binds in a serving tree of its own.

5 · ONE FACE ~ the recorded switch act (waits on the signer and the realm shapes)

  • The slice builds session-contract.mem in its build order and face-proof.mem. A session wears the face; the vessel wears none. The switch acts on ONE session and closes and re-dials only that session's links under the incoming face's scoped proof.
  • Working-layer law the build owes: one working document per (face, wiki, slot kind); its address derives from a secret only that face's vessels hold. The working layer follows its wiki across recipe recomposition. A leaf with an admit and no seat writes nothing to face wikis until join completion. Vessel removal leaves the address standing; the gate and document membership refuse the removed vessel (operator ruling).
  • A wiki names one face's composition over bags; sharing happens at the bag (operator ruling).
  • A personal Handle never rides without its owning face; one current personal Handle per PersonaGroup; a collective quorum Handle stays separate.
  • Every hand opens a session (operator ruling; lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/session-contract#/the-hands ): a tab, a CLI run, the disk watcher and a spirit's MCP seat differ by capability profile and never by key; MCP and the CLI stand as two doors onto one contract. The slice owes the watcher as a leasing session, the seat under a standing grant, the Mask as a record field, and door parity at the session. A tasked spirit comes down to an AI, a Mask and the vessel's seat, attended or standing; the vessel's own key serves as its system identity and the house mints no other.
  • The compose hunk in open-node-vessel.ts belongs to this slice's owner; cabal runtime work stops at its module boundary.

6 · TWO HEARTHS ~ the fresh start, then the ceremony (waits on everything above)

  • The fresh start by hand (#/fresh-start) on both machines, on the re-found build.
  • The ceremony: both found their seeds through the Stable act and stand; both found the Nexus fresh through the Nexus founding act; each signs a contract the two seats countersign; both join one shared cabal realm beside others; each wears a face; an edit crosses both ways and reads back after a restart on each machine. Probes use synthetic names; no fixture forges a cast persona's key or Handle.

The sealed store ~ five steps threaded through the paths (operator-approved model; step one's format stands as law and in code; each later step lands in its place before the fence, under #/the-attractor's ruling)

Step three's launch kit: lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/docs/train-s3-launch . * The model: cleartext lives in the live wiki and in the operator's projected files alone. A private bag rests as authenticated ciphertext chunks, the same bytes at rest and in a blind carrier's hands; a node that holds the capability and stands locked rests sealed. Private bytes take randomized authenticated encryption addressed over the ciphertext, so convergent sealing leaves the private tier. A bag's content key rolls at a causal removal act, never by a clock. * Scope (operator ruling): the sealing covers the wiki content stores, node and browser, and the content-addressed store, in carriage as well as at rest. The memory palace, the sensorium, receipts and logs stand outside it. Public and crossroads content stays readable to whoever holds the public keys or lookups. * Faces stand as facets of one operator (operator steer): no per-face projection root. Each kind of reader gets named — own vessel, own facet, a friend's face, a hosted guest, a carrier that holds none — before any admission-history rule gets written. One tree on disk serves the person: a projected wiki stands as a surface a session leases, and an edit from disk returns under that session's face ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/session-contract#/the-hands ). Whoever holds the disk reads every facet's projection and may link them. * Step 1, the one private-byte format: it stands as law at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/sealed-store , and that carrier names each part the code holds: the sealed value with its ciphertext address and named refusals; the key version, manifest and checkpoint records; the lockbox payload; shareable-history spans; the key lineage and the roll a seat roll feeds. The content-addressed store and the walker's carriage seal and open through it, no opener returns unauthenticated bytes, and no key derives from content or from a Nexus secret. The format changes ONCE, there. The content-key roll stands subscribed to the daemon island's seat rolls. What steps two to five take from the format reads at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/sealed-store#/owed . * Step 2, the key's seat: a content key's lockbox, the store that rests lockboxes, records and the chunk index, and the content-key holder stand as law at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/sealed-store#/the-store , and that carrier names each part the code holds. A node vessel's daemon island seats the holder beside its Keyhive identities and subscribes the content-key roll to its own seat rolls; a removal's successor wraps for the readers that remain, each lockbox into a Keyhive document of its own, so a removed device opens nothing a vessel seals after it counts the raise. Owed from this step, each to its owner: the session open that hands the island a signer and the chain check, without which no content key mints on a production vessel; a door from the island to the vessel's fold for the raises it counts; the face join's call for a from-now admission or a hand-over; and the browser shore's rest and Archive writer. A raise over the vessel that founded a face's key group names no seat a roll can leave out, so that vessel keeps its keys until K12 stands a fresh group. What steps three and four take from this step reads at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/sealed-store#/owed . * Step 3, after ONE FACE: sealed storage values and hydration scoped by the session's face; every fetch, write, catalog lookup and content-addressed read gets checked against that face, since a key alone cannot stop a trusted daemon handing one face another's document. * Step 4, after the KAHU PLANE's seats and wire: sealed history published to and fetched from a blind carrier by the house's own carriage protocol, with a causal index. A carrier never joins document sync. * Step 5, after ONE FACE: a public bag served at the floor of a locked node; today the floor serves the oracle snapshot alone. * History at admission (operator ruling): sharing a bag's past with a newcomer must have stood declared when those edits got written. An inviter chooses between history the realm already marked shareable and a present-state admission, and never widens the audience for another writer's past. The design target keeps one logical bag name whose key rolls mark epochs: a removal rolls, a from-now admission rolls, and a with-its-past admission hands over the earlier epochs' keys. * Mutual removal (operator ruling; lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/kapae ): both signed acts and both key branches stay, every member sees the conflict, and it ends in an explicit fork unless the realm's charter named a recovery council.

The shore ~ the browser's five rungs, beside the paths

  • The rungs: 0, an admitted crossing; 1, a carrier the node holds arrives and renders; 2, a typed field edit reaches the node and survives its restart, read from a fresh browser profile; 3, one human, many faces; 4, two hearths. Rungs 0–2 give the one-machine witness the working-Lararium ruling asks for, and the node's own read decides each rung, never a screenshot or a connected socket.
  • What blocks rungs 0–2: rungs 0 and 1 pass live (tools/browser-ci.sh, the crossing and render walks); rung 2's leaf half saves and survives a reload of the same profile. The working layer (a realm workbench, #/rulings 2026-11-11) and the durability receipt on the authenticated session stand built (F5; the workbench lane), so the node half now waits on a walk that reads it: the hearth's stage walk (L4) reads rung 2 whole, page to disk and back, on a node that serves its own page (L1) to a page that dials its own origin (L2).
  • Device capabilities get measured by tools/capability-probe/ on real devices, never assumed.
  • What blocks rung 3 onward: rung 3 waits on ONE FACE. Rung 4 waits on HARBOR's household HTTPS, on the KAHU PLANE's grant for a foreign leaf, and on browser custody: a browser leaf holds a passphrase or paper slot before it holds a persona root (#/rulings).
  • The walks ride tools/browser-ci.sh, and build under lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/dreamdom#/the-ruling: a frame carries absolute state, and a worker defines no global window.

The test re-cut ~ train T beside the paths (items in #/stride-t-recut; kit lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/test-recut-road )

  • What it builds: the house's code made testable at the grain its claims live at, before the founding (#/rulings, 2026-10-31): a shared scratch fixture, events where tests poll, pure readers lifted out of the boot composer, instance-owned doors, the composer cut along capability lines into stages both vessel kinds share, and a mutation runner that scores what a test proves.
  • Four waves: wave 0 holds the security, custody and correctness cures the cut reads found, each red-first, and a red that stays green retires its item; wave A runs parallel and touches no composer; wave B pins the boot's order and its events; wave C cuts the seams, and T12, the composer's stage-cut, runs as the one named single-owner step after T6's characterization pins.
  • It never stalls a path: a T item reaches the compose hunks of open-node-vessel.ts and open-browser-vessel.ts only through a module boundary or through T12's window. The kit holds the measured ground, the god-method's lift map, the floor's talk-story, the reads behind the items and the laws each item keeps; this carrier holds the rulings and the items.

Off this road, by ruling

  • Cloud carriers: capability and carrier law, private sealed carriage, per-store lifecycle, causal wake and its measurement. Deferred until the operator can deploy; the reading of "delegated keys" (a narrow carriage grant, or a backup that can act) stays open with them.
  • The Sensorium: waits behind the working-Lararium milestone. The memory stage (L8 to L13) runs on plain tiddlers with no embedding and no sidecar, and lifts nothing of this wait (#/rulings, 2026-10-31). Its return waits on G48 (a rewound turn stays recallable) and retires G47's three placeholders.
  • Streams (saqimtiaz/streams, BSD-3-Clause): a deferred crossroads offering; it never rides the genesis boot set.

The loops ~ the strides that hold every open item

No item rests here: each sits in the stride below that holds it, and a citation names that stride. Each item names what stays owed, the train of #/the-trains that closes it, and who owes a ruling first (operator, house, or nobody). An item names its file so a builder reads the code before leaning on the item; a line number drifts, so none rests here. Train letters: L the memory-stage hearth · T the test re-cut · S the custody seal · G ground · K the seat · N the Nexus founding act · P the Kahu plane · W the wire and the face proof · E the ember · O1 ONE FACE's sessions · H the hosted road · A HARBOR's tail · V recovery's verbs · B the browser and the phones · X TWO HEARTHS · C the contract leaps of 2026-11-14 (#/the-leaps) · R block and inline sigils at parsing parity (#/o-takiage; its R-prefixed namesakes, R-CHARTER and its kin, name the re-found's stages). DreamDOM's train D keeps its items in its own roadmap (#/side-lanes).

The strides serve as the plan the sprint overran (operator, 2026-11-13: "The 'Strides' served as the current milestones ahead, before we lightning-sprinted in the scratch directly (moved into the repo as scratch/ history)."). The strides name the milestones as planned; the sprint's planning records rest in the repository's scratch/ directory ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/history ), which informs and never governs. A stride item reads as owed only where the tree still lacks what it names; the tree and git decide, and an item the tree has outrun leaves its stride at the next true-up.

  • #/stride-0-hearth — the memory-stage hearth: the joined walk, the stage, the seat.

Stride 0 Hearth

The memory-stage hearth (#/the-attractor; #/rulings, 2026-10-31): the joined walk first, then the stage on it, then the move. Each item runs red-first with its control; an item's harness half walks the test door, and the operator's hand performs each founding and custody act (#/cold-start).

Reading note: the tree and git decide what stands (#/the-loops). Each item names the shape the tree holds today and what stays owed on it; landed work reads in git and #/ashes.

Seven-league stride notes

  • The stage's shape. The stage rests in the face's own daemon wiki and climbs by two crossings: the working top, where a stage meme stands under lar:///ha.ka.ba/kahua/memory/<class>/<slug>, its path carried by title and uri-path; the face's local canon bags/daemon-<F>, unsigned, by lares kahua stage; and the signed stage bags/kahua-<F>, by the hand's lift ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/kahua-memory-stage#/as-built ). Stage memes stay out of git (#/rulings, the memory stage's meme path). The stage scout's lean (two rounds, 2026-10-31) stays a lean the controller rules through L8: a stage/* tag family beside lifecycle/*, so an unpoured meme never enters the governed count and a meme poured as a carrier takes lifecycle/* at the pour; one index view; survivors reaching bags/ carriers by promotion; consolidation through a sitting, and through a talk-story floor where a stage meme contradicts held canon; no stage meme reads a clock.
  • Where the operator's hearth stands: the house as a container puts all code in the image (#/rulings), and tools/lares-container.sh stands it on the one house root LARES_HOUSE names, bound at /lar.
  • The operator's hand on the hearth, each when its item stands: move the old homes aside and found fresh by hand (#/fresh-start; #/rulings, 2026-10-31, a single operator's re-found); unlock; bind the browser's passphrase slot; carry the fleet-join offer, request, answer and land; run lares home tend from the main clone. None of these gates a lane.

The items

  • F11 The hands: the watcher as a leasing session, the seat under a standing grant, the Mask as a record field, door parity at the session ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/session-contract#/the-hands ). An agent's dissent drafts and a person's direct ask edits (#/rulings, 2026-10-16). O1. The four profiles stand in the session contract (HAND_PROFILES, session-contract.ts), and a session seated seat refuses the hand-only verbs at the socket by name: the kahua operator verbs beyond-profile, the promote and kāpae verbs promote-needs-hand and kapae-needs-hand (uds-channel.ts, store-door-verbs.ts). That refusal reads advisory, since a client names its own hand. Stride T Recut

The test re-cut (#/rulings, 2026-10-31; #/the-road, train T; kit lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/test-recut-road ). Reading note: the tree and git decide what stands (#/the-loops). Each item names the shape the tree holds today and what stays owed on it; landed work reads in git and #/ashes.

Wave 0 ~ the cures the cut reads found, ahead of any cut

Each item here stands confirmed by two readers from source; each item's first commit runs its red, and a red that stays green retires the item or its clause.

  • T21 Cabal invites run more than once (security). cabalInviteId names a seen-cache that no code builds; only the epoch lease expires an invite, so a removed joiner re-presents it until the epoch advances. The law stands: a removed joiner never re-enters on an invite minted before the removal; the red decides the mechanism (#/the-floor). Red: remove a joiner, re-present the invite inside the same epoch, assert refusal.
  • T23 An admitted browser leaf bricks on reload (correctness, fails closed). The admit bootstrap keeps the hearth gate key but drops the island kind and scope; later boots pass a null anchor, the island falls to the leaf's own key, and the Binding Gate throws "chain absent". A same-tab reboot hides it; a reload exposes it; it bites when the gate key stands. Node cures the same case by re-reading its pin each boot. Red: admit, then boot again with no admit, assert the same island key; controls: a null-gate admit, a founded leaf. The cure reads no old bootstrap shape.
  • T24 The browser catalog orphans wikis (correctness). After a data-amnesia re-found the browser never rewrites its catalog pointer, so every later boot re-founds a blank catalog and orphans the wikis registered since; the first boot also writes the pointer before a store flush. Red: boot twice on a missing catalog document, assert the second boot stays quiet; cure includes a flush before the boot keys write.
  • T25 A failed wiki-switch flips the browser surface. The browser handler tests the result object, always truthy, where node reads .ok. T13's shared switcher cures it; the red lands first.
  • T26 A herm registers wiki verbs it cannot run. They throw a TypeError on use (the CLI shore reaches wiki-act); the wiki verbs register behind the wiki capability.
  • T28 Wax-stamp verification owes its test. verifyWaxStampSig and mintWaxStamp stand with no caller and fail closed; the test rides W30.
  • T29 Unborn seats state their law. peerContractNymMap (the realm consult's root map, membership-doctrine#/two-maps) and walkerBoard (the reader half of the owed E walker arm) read as unborn seats, never dead code: their comments restate as law and name their owed items. A seat with no road item and no canon behind it burns.
  • T37 The keyslot's key hygiene (custody, security read). The KDF half stands (lane kdf-native): the passphrase KDF rides a minted port at one cost (native scrypt on the host, noble in the browser, identical bytes); a pin derives a passphrase only when it tries it and stops at the first share, so a right first guess pays one derivation where it paid three; each distinct passphrase counts as one attempt, charged before the open and refunded only on an open or a reading that ran no derivation; a face founding derives before its index entry lands; a KDF refusal zeroes the keys the unlock already opened. Owed, each red-first with its control: the keyslot never zeroes its scrypt material, KEK, slot key or shares (keyslot.ts); doUnlock leaks the tree key and secret key, and doUnlockFace the face key, when the root carrier's open throws, and the CLI's proof leaves its tree key unzeroed; a route keys on encoded bytes, or refuses a passphrase isWellFormed rejects, since lone surrogates collapse today; the door types kdf-refused as its refusal reason, where it reads act-failed; the window where a face's index entry stands before its key stores stays narrowed, never closed. The lazy open's timing reads as a MEDIUM oracle the day any remote unlock or proof door stands. S.

Wave A ~ parallel, none touches the composer

  • T2 The shared scratch fixture: its codemod.   • T4 The dead-export sweep, regenerated by a whole-tree token index: 67 names occur once in the tree. 35 burn; 12 carry a value another file re-spells inline, and those get imported where they belong rather than deleted (the node carrier-name regexes vault-carriers.ts re-spells); 14 wait on rulings; 6 stand (serveHostingMint for train H, the two SIBLING_*_KIND constants, the two session-contract compile-time witnesses, TW5SyncAdaptor). Stride 1 Spine

Face and custody ground, then sealed identity and private authority; the face anchor precedes the planes that inherit it.

Reading note: the tree and git decide what stands (#/the-loops). Each item names the shape the tree holds today and what stays owed on it; landed work reads in git and #/ashes.

Seven-league stride notes

  • Integrity owed: face isolation, end to end (K15 step 3, B1). Its witness: one adversarial multi-tab, multi-face run in a real browser, an unadmitted port, a stale generation, a cross-face sealed write and the last session's lock. The guards and their reds stand on fake islands and the node, and the one SharedWorker per house stands (open-browser-vessel.ts, shared-holder-production.test.ts on every engine that attaches one); the real-browser adversarial run stays owed.
  • Identity authority enforced, beyond the prose (K15 step 6). The anchor's suite holds it (packages/lararium-mesh/tests/face-anchor.test.ts): a forged selector and a coherent foreign bundle refuse, one sequence signed twice surfaces as a fork, and a frontier never falls. What stays owed beyond it rests in lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/k15-wave-c-lane#/owed .

Stride 2 Floor

Floor, wire and realm release shapes on the face and authority ground.

Reading note: the tree and git decide what stands (#/the-loops). Each item names the shape the tree holds today and what stays owed on it; landed work reads in git and #/ashes.

Seven-league stride notes

Stride 3 Bake

The one labelled re-bake and rename, followed by the founding format cut.

Reading note: the tree and git decide what stands (#/the-loops). Each item names the shape the tree holds today and what stays owed on it; landed work reads in git and #/ashes.

Seven-league stride notes

  • Integrity owed: the bake's cross-product (X0, touching K15 step 6, W26, G37). Its witness: one matrix over the pre-bake and post-bake artifacts, every old CID refused after the bake and a fresh two-of-two founding standing, rather than greens read item by item.
  • Integrity owed: the habit flip at the bake (X0, G37). Before the bake the house runs re-genesis freely, since no CRDT document survives; after it, a re-genesis costs every CID a second operator pins. Its witness: a census of each instrument, fixture, brief and runbook line that assumes a free re-genesis, each named to flip or retire inside the bake's own commit, never after it.

    • The persona-root matcher: the pattern no longer matches a leading-zero index (-h01); confirm no writer emits one, else widen the matcher.

    • A runaway inside one file (VSCode-TW5-Syntax tools/composition-check.js, Lares-Awakens' side): the check misses a runaway that starts inside one file, so the seed read zero failures while broken (2026-11-01); a planted single-file runaway reds it first.
    • VSCode-TW5-Syntax 3.0.3 (deferred past the 3.0.2 release; Lares-Awakens' list rides the submodule's branch): on the house side, api/mu.mem line 14 writes ->>>, which reads clearer as -> >>, a canon re-cut the house owns; on the submodule's side, the rest of the list, with the long-standing multi-line ~~ strikethrough divergence (older than R) beside it.
    • The holder venv's cache key (L3's tail): ci.yml and e2e-shards.yml key the cached ~/.venv on mempalace/pyproject.toml and the sensorium requirements alone, so a mempalace submodule commit or a Python patch release reuses a stale venv; the key takes the submodule's commit and the interpreter's full version.
    • The veil reader: packages/lararium-sensorium/scripts/worldline_veil.py expects a persona root in the clear at the identity root, while roots now rest encrypted per face, so it reads nothing; a sensorium porch item.
    • The carried-Nexus rig's notes (T19's and T27's tails): T19's chmod 000 case bites nothing under root (a false red alone); seatStranger leaks its dial when the seat fails; T27's rig waits a fixed 3 s settle window.
    • Faces whose keys open after boot (T33's tail): a face the vessel holds but does not wear gets its ledger classed vessel-private only when it mounts, so its frontier pins and seen entries stay reachable through a realm alias until then (its marks still need receipts); the cure reads atom keys before custody opens.

Off this road, held by name

  • Grammar: tangle, the putback law and the tongue fold wait on a real return trip and a whole regeneration of packages/tree-sitter-memetic-wikitext against the released grammar; that regeneration also unlocks publication. A relay-stream profile (the SYN-framed batch), a carrier seal with a computed check, and EOT targeting stay held in reserve. The grammar watch reads in lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/grammar-watch .
  • Publication: the text/markdown; variant=memetic-markdown registration, the provisional lar scheme after lar-uri gains its Security section, the vendor media type, and the drafts. The standalone plugin artifact ships from packages/lararium-tw5/plugins/standalone/.
  • The live wiki's floors: exercise the quoteblock floor at both grains, the child gate and the persisted parallel drafts against real concurrent browser saves, and turn every surprise into a test.
  • A standing whole-tree gate runs as a spirit pass, never as a CLI verb or a trigger.
  • The ahi-kā knobs (the hysteresis gap, the commons-against-hearth exemption) wait on an estimator fit; no ruling stands owed.
  • Future vessels (native mobile, Mudlet, Unreal) enter one at a time, each declaring whether it hosts a sovereign vessel or attaches a surface to a named one. Each enters as a DreamDOM surface: a root template over a document sink, its two-way capability rated by name ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/dreamdom#/the-superset ), on train D's lean or wider phases.

The fresh start ~ by hand, reversible, never a command

NO pave or wipe command stands (operator-ruled): such a command carries too much risk. In early alpha a fresh START still reads pono when it runs BY HAND: MOVE the old homes aside — rename them, never delete them, so the move stays reversible — then found fresh on the re-found build. Valuable data lives in git, in the private claude-backup repository, or on D:; a vessel home holds nothing that only it holds.

A fresh machine needs no move: a fresh vessel seeds from the tracked bags/ and the committed genesis/ (lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/founding-runbook#/fresh-state).

The homes to move: the ones the OLD build's resolvers name — the ~/.lares home, the XDG data homes for lares and lararium, and the state home — asked of the old build at the moment of moving, never enumerated by hand. A founding refuses a store that holds carriers no slot tree commits to (OccupiedStoreRefusal, before any route gets asked and before any write), which keeps an accidental founding off any home left unmoved. Other Nexuses rejoin through presented admits; every old admit names an old AID and reads dead by construction.

Open ~ what the operator owns

Seats, custody and recovery

  1. Recovery contacts and recovery. The enrolment shape stands ruled (#/rulings). Request authentication, the veto, a person's death and succession, the catastrophe rite and any third key, and a Handle's DNS binding stand open at lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/kahu#/seed-and-nexus .
  2. A sealed receipt manifest for carriage. It would let a root restored from paper re-open its carriage after device loss — and would turn carriage toward the backup W rules it never becomes. The lenses split; re-opening W needs the operator's turned key.
  3. Carriage across leaves. A guest-held carry-claim proven at the carry door would let carriage follow a guest across leaves — a second relation and a second domain. Lean: not built.
  4. The reading of "delegated keys" for cloud carriers, deferred until the operator can deploy to a cloud surface (#/the-road).
  5. After-founding comms for non-Claude seats: lane-message tiddlers on the daemon wiki, read and written through lane capabilities, ride #/the-order, rank 31, and never gate a founding.
  6. A kāpae over a top-only title (L37, recipe-layer-model#/delete-reveals-or-shadows). A kāpae over a title that stood ONLY in the top layer hides any copy that later arrives beneath. Default, carried until the operator rules: kāpae stays the default everywhere, since it guards against a concurrent copy resurrecting the record and every door already raises one; the consequence names the hiding, and L38's lower door gives a hand the way back.
  7. The confirm gate's shape (#/rulings, 2026-11-13). The operator's note asks the gate; the house's lean names its shape (the passphrase where the vessel holds custody, the typed phrase at a bare floor, the platform's own clear beyond any house door, the class as every door that destroys a place or a face). The browser stands built as leaned (C4); a locked vessel verifies a passphrase by the unwrap alone against the trees at rest. Open beside it: the passkey and paper routes at the gate, and whether any tree that opens narrows to the place tree.
  8. House leans the operator may overturn: the floor vessel key drops its next-key commitment; the long-silent device rule; the strangers' dial drops the newest arrival (#/decisions); the seal's standing leans (S7 of #/stride-1-spine).

Planes and offerings

  1. The offering's offer and witness caps against the Lamplighter stack.
  2. The crossing record. Its durable emission threshold (lean: authority, vessel, federation and externally meaningful-effect boundaries alone), and whether a destination receipt always signs separately (lean: yes).
  3. Engine cadence: who holds a cadence share and the epoch pin stand ruled (#/rulings); whether partial conformance lands before kahuli engine un-holds ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/kahu#/contract ).
  4. Grammar submissions: whether the Nexus Kahu or the corpus-kahu receive them.
  5. DNS custody past the credential: delegated _acme-challenge, record custody, the ACME account and CAA travel as one question ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/bounded-reach-contract ). With it: whether a host timer or a hand runs renewal, and the answer for a phone that forces encrypted DNS.
  6. The crossroads mirror's filter (G21 of #/operator-gates).
  7. Removal's reach. The model stands ruled (#/rulings; lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/api/pono/kapae#/four-edges ). Five questions stay the operator's, each with the house's lean. Whether an edge act may ever use the emergency class (lean: never). Whether a seated member under a member shadow keeps a seat's signature until the next roll (lean: yes, a seat leaves by a roll alone). What a recovery council holds after a mutual removal (lean: nothing built; the fork stands as the one exit). Whether a holder's key reads at its log's current head, so a recovered face keeps its edges (lean: yes, owed with the resolver of #/side-lanes). Whether a counted raise moves the keys while a lift still races it (lean: yes, remove-wins reaches the keys, and a lift then seats afresh). Two more came with the key side. What removes a seat no standing hand can revoke, the founding vessel's own among them (lean: a fresh group, at the re-founding that moves the PersonaGroup to a Keyhive group). Whether a vessel that holds no revoke authority goes on trying a raise until it sees the seat leave (lean: yes, and each try hands the roll on again, so the content key rolls whoever revokes the seat).
  8. The self-slot inner ring. Wiring it freezes a naming: a bag URL through a live ring hashes to seed its document, and a re-name costs a re-founding. Lean: widen the bridge first, and wire the ring on an explicit ruling alone.

Shore, grammar and code

  1. The ungated slot child. A slot child's native save never grades; three child-edit shapes stand marked as the operator's call (packages/lararium-tw5/tests/meme-backstop.test.ts; lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/syncer-back-parity ).
  2. Ruling-gated mesh modules (listed in the header of packages/lararium-mesh/src/index.ts): recipe, readiness, reaction-graph, pronaos, offering-inspection. Each moves or stays by ruling alone.
  3. The capture cell sits apart for review in packages/lararium-mesh/src/capture/, packages/lararium-node/src/capture/, packages/lararium-tw5/src/capture/ and packages/lares-cli/src/commands/capture/. Canon places capture in Python per stream with the TS daemon coordinating alone ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/api/capture-annotation-model ), while the TS cell carries the live path. Rule: retire it to Python, or re-home it.
  4. Receipt names and verb capabilities. Whether a receipt's address follows the house's own kind nouns or the content-addressed surface; and the table that maps each routed verb to its capability, where deriveRoutedCap answers for every routed verb today (packages/lararium-tw5/src/verb-local-dispatch.ts).
  5. Rulings that rest in their own carriers: the promotion receipt's fields ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/canon-boundary ), the one-of-one mantle ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/handle-card ), the lease's questions ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/realm-bag-brief ), and the offering's questions ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lares/docs/pono/heraldry ).
  6. Two Kairoses. The fiction names KAIROS as a watcher that speaks unasked; the code's Kairos pet-names an unbuilt entity that refreshes an OAuth grant at the right moment ( lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/reach-plane ). One entity, or two that share a name?
  7. Alerts and shadows. Whether a disk conflict that arrives while no wiki stands pinned raises its alert at the next pin; whether a hand that deletes its override of a shadow leaves a delete, where today the shadow's text gets saved as a record; whether the page warns when a browser has joined no face.
  8. The confirm door upstream. The fork asks its questions through the wiki itself ($tw.utils.confirm, tm-confirm). Owed before a donation: the order of actions after a confirm, a switch for a blocking prompt, and one squashed change. The withdrawn render boundary's upstream pull request, if it still stands open, advertises a design the fork backed out.
  9. The realm consult's root map (peerContractNymMap, open-node-vessel.ts): a seat held by lar:///ha.ka.ba/lararium/mesh/membership-doctrine#/two-maps and the house ruling of 2026-10-16 (#/rulings, a realm's steward map holds leaves), filled only by a root edge proven at the seat. Nothing fills it today and its readers fail closed. The house leans keep it and name an owed item for its filler (T29 states its law).
  10. The barrel re-exported names (487): they wait on their own ruling before T4's sweep reaches them.
  11. One sentence for the CLAUDE.md adapter note, the operator's to place: the CLI's ingest, watch and REPACK derive against LAR_BAGS and LAR_WIKIS, so "read neither" holds for the daemon's projection mirrors alone.
